# CORE-CAP-SPECIALTY — consolidated source profiles

Uses an existing registered canonical owner and preserves all prior addenda. Previously inactive registered owners receive their first source-preserving specification without creating a new architecture owner. Every clause below applies only to its listed source profiles; it is not a universal clinical rule. No new service, clinical approval or implementation readiness.

## pediatric-neuro-sensory-dental-functional

Specialty owns longitudinal clinical care coordination and source-scoped anatomical/functional projections, not a copy of Medication, Laboratory, Imaging, AI or Twin. Preserve functional classification, bilateral eye/hearing records, tooth-level primary/permanent dentition and appliance lifecycle, trauma-sensitive narratives and caregiver/family boundaries. ROP screening dates and missed follow-up escalation cannot become a generic adult schedule.

Acceptance additions: Preserve every original clause, patient/tenant/source/version/time provenance and explicit missingness. Test wrong identity, stale measurements, revoked consent, guardian changes, adolescent confidentiality and source corrections. Pediatric age, developmental stage, weight/height/unit/time and clinical context remain explicit where applicable. No automatic diagnosis, prescription, dose change, medication stop, device actuation or crisis disposition; qualified clinician review remains required. Clinical validation and production acceptance remain external gates.

## pediatric-combined-specialty-safety

Specialty owns coordinated medication/device/procedure/infection safety views; Medication retains drug exposure, Devices retains implanted/external equipment, Encounter retains surgery. A combined alert is not a new parallel prescribing or equipment platform. Preserve overdue device removal/review, infection history, resistant organisms, steroid/renal context and prophylaxis as separately attributed requirements.

## pediatric-development-and-quality-of-life

Pediatric coordination preserves validated instrument/version/age applicability, respondent identity, development domains, seizures, joints, infection recovery, bone age/DXA growth context, rehabilitation and cross-specialty dashboards. Patient-reported quality of life remains distinct from clinical assessment. Growth/anatomical/AI outputs cannot silently become verified findings; every source-specific requirement and specialist handoff remains traceable.

## maternal-newborn-longitudinal-care

Maternal/newborn profiles retain linked but distinct patient identities, gestational dating basis, fetal growth reference version, CTG acquisition, labor stage, birth preferences, procedures, APGAR timing, recovery and lactation. Contraction timers and mood screens do not triage autonomously; urgent concerns require explicit human escalation. AI never changes delivery plans or treats fetal and maternal facts as interchangeable.

## skin-and-sensitive-infection-care

Skin and infection profiles retain lesion site/photo chronology, severity instruments, flares, treatment and laboratory correction context. Sensitive STI/HIV data require segmented permissions; advertised anonymous testing is jurisdiction-dependent and cannot be linked back without lawful authorization. Cancer screening is not diagnosis.
